Our work demonstrates that LLDMs can deliver both fast inference and reliable hallucination detection simultaneously.

</details>


### [142] [EndPrompt: Efficient Long-Context Extension via Terminal Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14589)
*Han Tian,Luxuan Chen,Xinran Chen,Rui Kong,Fang Wang,Jiamin Chen,Jinman Zhao,Yuchen Li,Jiashu Zhao,Shuaiqiang Wang,Haoyi Xiong,Dawei Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EndPrompt方法，仅使用短训练序列即可实现大语言模型上下文窗口的有效扩展（如从8K到64K），通过在短序列中保留完整第一段并附加具有目标长距离位置索引的简短终端提示，实现长距离相对位置暴露，无需全长度训练，在RULER和LongBench上超越全长度微调等方法，且计算成本大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统长上下文扩展需要训练完整目标长度的序列，导致二次方级的显存和计算开销，使得长上下文适配昂贵且难以复现。作者试图探索是否可以通过稀疏的位置监督而非密集的长序列训练来实现上下文窗口的扩展。

Method: 提出EndPrompt方法：将原始短上下文作为完整的第一段保留，附加一个简短的终端提示作为第二段，并为其分配接近目标上下文长度的位置索引。这种两段式结构在短物理序列中同时引入局部和长距离相对位置距离，同时保持训练文本的语义连续性。基于旋转位置编码（RoPE）和Bernstein不等式提供了理论分析，证明位置插值对注意力函数施加了严格的平滑性约束，共享的Transformer参数进一步抑制了对未观测中间距离的不稳定外推。

Result: 在LLaMA系列模型上将上下文窗口从8K扩展到64K，EndPrompt在RULER上平均得分76.03，在LongBench上获得最高平均分，超越LCEG（72.24）、LongLoRA（72.95）和全长度微调（69.23），同时计算量大幅减少。

Conclusion: 长上下文泛化可以从稀疏的位置监督中诱导出来，挑战了"密集长序列训练是可靠上下文窗口扩展的必要条件"的主流假设。代码已开源。

Abstract: Extending the context window of large language models typically requires training on sequences at the target length, incurring quadratic memory and computational costs that make long-context adaptation expensive and difficult to reproduce. We propose EndPrompt, a method that achieves effective context extension using only short training sequences. The core insight is that exposing a model to long-range relative positional distances does not require constructing full-length inputs: we preserve the original short context as an intact first segment and append a brief terminal prompt as a second segment, assigning it positional indices near the target context length. This two-segment construction introduces both local and long-range relative distances within a short physical sequence while maintaining the semantic continuity of the training text--a property absent in chunk-based simulation approaches that split contiguous context. We provide a theoretical analysis grounded in Rotary Position Embedding and the Bernstein inequality, showing that position interpolation induces a rigorous smoothness constraint over the attention function, with shared Transformer parameters further suppressing unstable extrapolation to unobserved intermediate distances. Applied to LLaMA-family models extending the context window from 8K to 64K, EndPrompt achieves an average RULER score of 76.03 and the highest average on LongBench, surpassing LCEG (72.24), LongLoRA (72.95), and full-length fine-tuning (69.23) while requiring substantially less computation. These results demonstrate that long-context generalization can be induced from sparse positional supervision, challenging the prevailing assumption that dense long-sequence training is necessary for reliable context-window extension. The code is available at https://github.com/clx1415926/EndPrompt.

</details>


### [143] [SciPaths: Forecasting Pathways to Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14600)
*Eric Chamoun,Yizhou Chi,Yulong Chen,Rui Cao,Zifeng Ding,Michalis Korakakis,Andreas Vlachos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出发现路径预测任务，并构建SciPaths基准（262条专家标注+2,444条银级路径），评估发现从目标科学贡献反向推理使能贡献及前人工作依赖的能力。最强模型严格语义匹配下F1仅0.189，方法学依赖最难恢复，分解质量是端到端路径恢复的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI4Science基准主要关注引文预测、文献检索或想法生成，缺乏对科学进步所依赖的使能贡献序列（即贡献间依赖关系）的建模与评估。

Method: 定义发现路径预测任务：给定目标贡献和指定时间点的已有文献，模型需识别所需的使能贡献并将其锚定到前人工作。构建SciPaths基准，包含来自ML/NLP论文的262条专家标注金路径和2,444条银路径，每条路径记录使能贡献、角色、理由及前人工作锚定或未映射决策。

Result: 最佳模型在严格语义匹配下F1仅0.189，核心方法论依赖最难恢复。当提供金标准使能贡献时，前人工作锚定性能显著提升，表明分解质量是端到端路径恢复的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: SciPaths将评估转向科学预测中缺失的能力：从目标贡献反向推理使能科学构建块及前人工作依赖，揭示了当前模型在推理科学贡献依赖关系方面的显著不足。

Abstract: Scientific progress depends on sequences of enabling contributions, yet existing AI4Science benchmarks largely focus on citation prediction, literature retrieval, or idea generation rather than the dependencies that make progress possible. In this paper, we introduce discovery pathway forecasting: given a target scientific contribution and the prior literature available at a specified time, the task is to (1) identify the enabling contributions required to realize it and (2) ground each in prior work when such prior work exists. We present SciPaths, a benchmark of 262 expert-annotated gold pathways and 2,444 silver pathways constructed from machine learning and natural language processing papers, where each pathway records enabling contributions, roles, rationales, and prior-work groundings or unmapped decisions. Evaluating frontier and open-weight language models, we find that the best model reaches only 0.189 F1 under strict semantic matching, with core methodological dependencies hardest to recover. Prior-work grounding improves substantially when gold enabling contributions are provided, showing that decomposition quality is a major bottleneck for end-to-end pathway recovery. SciPaths therefore shifts evaluation toward a missing capability in scientific forecasting: reasoning backward from a target contribution to the enabling scientific building blocks and prior-work dependencies that make it feasible.

</details>


### [144] [AI-assisted cultural heritage dissemination: Comparing NMT and glossary-augmented LLM translation in rock art documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14679)
*Vicent Briva-Iglesias,María Ferre-Fernández*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种英译西设置（DeepL、Gemini-Simple、Gemini-RAG），发现在文化遗产领域（以岩画文本为例），基于术语检索增强的Gemini-RAG（准确率81.4%）显著优于无辅助的DeepL（64.4%）和Gemini-Simple（69.1%），且整体翻译质量相当，表明术语增强提示是一种低成本的术语控制方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产机构的多语种传播受限于预算和人力，在岩画等术语密集型领域，翻译中术语的准确性和一致性至关重要，小错误会误导非专业人士并降低文本复用率。现有方法多关注模型侧改进，本文探索简单可操作的术语干预方案。

Method: 对比三种英译西班牙语机器翻译设置：（1）DeepL强NMT基线、（2）Gemini-Simple（基础提示LLM）、（3）Gemini-RAG（同一LLM+术语对检索增强提示）。采用PEARMUT框架，通过多路直接评估（0-100分）和受限MQM分类学的术语审计进行人工评价。

Result: Gemini-RAG术语精确匹配准确率最高（81.4%），优于Gemini-Simple（69.1%）和DeepL（64.4%）；整体质量方面，Gemini-RAG平均DA得分85.3，与Gemini-Simple的85.2基本持平，均高于DeepL的80.3。

Conclusion: 术语增强提示是一种低开销的方法，可在文化遗产翻译中有效提升术语控制能力，前提是机构维护最低限度的术语资源和轻量级评估流程。

Abstract: Cultural heritage institutions increasingly disseminate research and interpretive materials globally, but multilingual dissemination is constrained by limited budgets and staffing. In terminology-dense domains such as rock art, translation quality depends on accurate, consistent specialised terms, and small lexical errors can mislead non-specialists and reduce reuse. We compare three English MT setups for a Spanish academic rock art text, focusing on simple, operationally feasible interventions rather than complex model-side modifications: (1) DeepL as a strong NMT baseline, (2) Gemini-Simple (LLM with a basic prompt), and (3) Gemini-RAG (the same LLM with glossary-augmented prompting via term-pair retrieval). Using PEARMUT, we conduct a human evaluation via (i) multi-way Direct Assessment (0--100) and (ii) targeted terminology auditing with a restricted MQM taxonomy. Gemini-RAG yields the highest exact-match terminology accuracy (81.4\%), versus Gemini-Simple (69.1\%) and DeepL (64.4\%), while preserving overall quality (mean DA 85.3 Gemini-RAG vs. 85.2 Gemini-Simple), outperforming DeepL (80.3). These results show that glossary-augmented prompting is a low-overhead way to improve terminology control in cultural-heritage translation if institutions maintain minimal terminology resources and lightweight evaluation procedures.

</details>


### [145] [Non-linear Interventions on Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14749)
*Sangwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的干预框架，将干预从线性表示假设扩展到非线性流形上的特征，并支持对隐式特征进行干预，在拒绝绕过引导任务上取得了比线性基线更精确的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的干预方法受限于线性表示假设，只能处理线性编码的特征，无法对非线性流形上编码的特征进行有效干预。

Method: 提出一种通用的干预公式，自然扩展到非线性表示的特征，并结合学习过程实现对缺乏直接输出签名的隐式特征的干预。

Result: 在拒绝绕过引导任务上，该方法比线性基线更精确地引导模型行为。

Conclusion: 该框架突破了线性干预的限制，能够处理非线性特征和隐式特征，实现了更精确的模型行为引导。

Abstract: Intervention is one of the most representative and widely used methods for understanding the internal representations of large language models (LLMs). However, existing intervention methods are confined to linear interventions grounded in the Linear Representation Hypothesis, leaving features encoded along non-linear manifolds beyond their reach. In this work, we introduce a general formulation of intervention that extends naturally to non-linearly represented features, together with a learning procedure that further enables intervention on implicit features lacking a direct output signature. We validate our framework on refusal bypass steering, where it steers the model more precisely than linear baselines by intervening on a non-linear feature governing refusal.

</details>


### [146] [Streaming Speech-to-Text Translation with a SpeechLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14766)
*Titouan Parcollet,Shucong Zhang,Xianrui Zheng,Rogier C. van Dalen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的实时流式语音到文本翻译架构，让模型自主决定何时输出翻译，仅需1-2秒延迟即可达到接近非流式基线的翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SpeechLLM系统无法实现真正的流式处理，要么等待完整音频后才输出翻译，要么以固定间隔输出token，不适合实时应用。

Method: 提出LLM-based架构，模型不仅学习输出翻译token，还学习判断是否已接收到足够音频来执行输出。训练中使用输入语音和输出文本的自动对齐。

Result: 在不同语言对上，系统实现了接近非流式基线的翻译质量，延迟仅为1-2秒。

Conclusion: 该架构能够有效地将流式能力融入SpeechLLM，在保证翻译质量的同时大幅降低延迟，适合实时应用场景。

Abstract: Normally, a system that translates speech into text consists of separate modules for speech recognition and text-to-text translation. Combining those tasks into a SpeechLLM promises to exploit paralinguistic information in the speech and to reduce cascaded errors. But existing SpeechLLM systems are slow since they do not work in a real streaming fashion: they wait for a complete utterance of audio before outputting a translation, or output tokens at fixed intervals, which is not suitable for real applications. This work proposes an LLM-based architecture for real streaming speech-to-text translation. The LLM learns not just to emit output tokens, but also to decide whether it has seen enough audio to do so. The system is trained using automatic alignments of the input speech and the output text. In experiments on different language pairs, the system achieves a translation quality close to the non-streaming baseline, but with a latency of only 1-2 seconds.

</details>


### [147] [Graphs of Research: Citation Evolution Graphs as Supervision for Research Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14790)
*Songyang Gao,Yinghui Xia,Siyi Liu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Graphs of Research (GoR) 方法，通过构建论文演化有向无环图（DAG）来监督微调 LLM，实现基于引用关系的科研想法自动生成，在 LLM 裁判锦标赛中达到 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 驱动的科研想法生成方法主要依赖静态检索或复杂提示工程，未能充分利用参考文献之间的结构关系。作者旨在通过引入引用演化图作为监督信号，提升 LLM 生成科研想法的质量。

Method: 提出 GoR 方法：1) 为每篇种子论文提取 2-hop 参考文献邻域；2) 从引用位置、引用频率、前驱链接和发表时间四个维度推导参考文献间的关系；3) 将这些关系组织成论文演化有向无环图（DAG）；4) 构建自动化提取流水线，从五大 ML/NLP 顶会收集数据（498/50/50 训练/验证/测试种子论文，约 7,600 篇被引文献）；5) 对 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-1M 进行监督微调，输入包含引用图、边信号、参考文献信息和任务定义的结构化文本提示，预测种子论文的研究想法。

Result: 在与 gpt-4o 驱动的基线方法进行头对头 LLM 裁判锦标赛中，GoR-SFT 达到了 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: 引用演化图作为 LLM 监督信号能有效提升科研想法生成质量，有望降低引用演化图使用的门槛，加速自动化科学创新。

Abstract: Research idea generation is the innovation-driving step of automated scientific research. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have shown potential for automating idea generation at scale. However, existing methods mainly condition LLMs on eliciting idea generation through static retrieval of relevant literature or complex prompt engineering, without discarding the structural relations among references. We propose Graphs of Research (GoR), a supervised fine-tuning method that extracts a 2-hop reference neighborhood for each seed paper, derives the relations among those references from citation position, frequency, predecessor links, and publication time, and organizes them into a paper-evolution directed acyclic graph (DAG). We construct an automated extraction pipeline that draws data from five major ML/NLP venues, comprising 498/50/50 train/validation/test seed papers and approximately 7,600 cited references. Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-1M is fine-tuned on a structured-text prompt that includes the citation graph, edge signals, reference information, and task definition to predict the idea for the seed paper. Across head-to-head LLM-judge tournaments against gpt-4o-driven baselines, GoR-SFT achieves SOTA, demonstrating the effectiveness of citation-evolution graphs as supervision signal for LLM-based idea generation. We hope that this reduces the barrier for citation evolution graphs as a supervision, accelerating automated scientific innovation.

</details>


### [148] [Conversion of Lexicon-Grammar tables to LMF. Application to French](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14816)
*Eric Laporte,Elsa Tolone,Mathieu Constant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将法语动词的词典-语法表转换为LMF格式，分析了转换过程中的主要困难，并描述了最终资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法语词典-语法是法语词汇和句法信息的主要来源，但其原始格式缺乏互操作性。将其转换为LMF标准格式，可使其在不同自然语言处理环境中可用，促进词典的标准化和互操作性。

Method: 作者将法语动词的词典-语法表转换为LMF格式，分析了转换过程中面临的主要困难，并描述了生成的资源。

Result: 成功实现了法语动词词典-语法表到LMF格式的首次转换实验，生成了符合LMF标准的互操作资源。

Conclusion: 将词典-语法转换为LMF标准格式是可行的，该转换有助于法语词汇资源的标准化和NLP领域的互操作性。

Abstract: We describe the first experiment of conversion of Lexicon-Grammar tables for French verbs into the Lexical Markup Framework (LMF) format. The Lexicon-Grammar of the French language is currently one of the major sources of lexical and syntactic information for French. Its conversion into an interoperable representation format according to the LMF standard makes it usable in different contexts, thus contributing to the standardization and interoperability of natural language processing dictionaries. We briefly introduce the Lexicon-Grammar and the derived dictionaries; we analyse the main difficulties faced during the conversion; and we describe the resulting resource.

</details>


### [149] [Tokenizer Fertility and Zero-Shot Performance of Foundation Models on Ukrainian Legal Text: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14890)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了7种基础模型在乌克兰法律文本上的分词效率和零样本性能，发现分词效率差异达1.6倍，NVIDIA Nemotron Super 3（120B）以更低成本超越参数规模大5.6倍的Mistral Large 3，且少样本提示在形态丰富语言中会显著降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对基础模型在乌克兰法律文本领域分词效率与性能的系统性对比，而分词效率直接影响API成本，少样本学习在形态丰富语言中的有效性也尚未在司法领域得到验证。

Method: 选取来自5个提供商的7种模型，使用乌克兰国家登记处（EDRSR）的273份经过验证的法院判决，分别测量分词器的"生育率"（tokenizer fertility）以及在三个任务上的零样本和少样本性能，并进行分层消融和提示敏感性分析。

Result: (1) 分词效率差异达1.6倍，Qwen3模型比Llama家族多消耗60% token；(2) NVIDIA Nemotron Super 3（120B）综合得分83.1，优于Mistral Large 3（675B总参数量/41B活跃参数量），API成本仅为后者1/3；(3) 少样本提示使性能最多下降26个百分点，经消融实验确认这是乌克兰语演示本身的固有问题。

Conclusion: 实践中应优先进行分词器分析再选择模型；对于形态丰富的语言，零样本比少样本更可靠。

Abstract: Foundation models tokenize Ukrainian legal text with vastly different efficiency, yet no systematic comparison exists for this domain. We benchmark seven models from five providers on 273 validated court decisions from Ukraine's state registry (EDRSR), measuring tokenizer fertility and zero-shot performance on three tasks. Three findings emerge. (1) Tokenizer fertility varies 1.6x: Qwen3 models consume 60% more tokens than Llama-family models on identical input, directly reducing API cost. (2) NVIDIA Nemotron Super 3 (120B) achieves the highest composite score (83.1), outperforming Mistral Large 3 (675B total, 41B active) -- a model with 5.6x more total parameters and 3.4x more active parameters per token -- at one-third the API cost. (3) Few-shot prompting degrades performance by up to 26 percentage points; stratified and prompt-sensitivity ablations confirm this is intrinsic to Ukrainian-language demonstrations, not an artifact of example selection. For practitioners: tokenizer analysis should precede model selection, and zero-shot is a more reliable default than few-shot for morphologically rich languages.

</details>


### [150] [Chain-of-Procedure: Hierarchical Visual-Language Reasoning for Procedural QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14928)
*Guanhua Chen,Yutong Yao,Shenghe Sun,Ci-Jun Gao,Shudong Liu,Lidia S. Chao,Feng Wan,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ProcedureVQA基准测试评估VLM在视觉程序问答（VP-QA）中的能力，并针对其存在的跨模态检索不足和图文粒度不匹配问题，提出Chain-of-Procedure（CoP）层级推理框架，在六个VLM上实现最高13%的绝对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然VLM在标准图像-文本任务上表现优异，但它们在视觉程序问答（VP-QA）这一实际任务中的潜力尚未被充分探索。VP-QA要求用户上传中间状态图片来查询下一步操作，现有VLM在此场景下存在能力不足。

Method: 提出Chain-of-Procedure（CoP）层级推理框架，该框架包含三个步骤：首先利用视觉线索检索相关指令，然后通过语义分解进行步骤细化，最后生成下一步操作。

Result: 在六个VLM上的实验表明，CoP框架相比标准基线实现了最高13%的绝对性能提升。

Conclusion: ProcedureVQA有效揭示了VLM在视觉程序推理中的两个关键局限（跨模态检索不足和图文粒度不匹配），而CoP框架通过层级推理有效缓解了这些问题，为视觉程序问答任务提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved impressive results on standard image-text tasks, yet their potential for visual procedure question answering (VP-QA) remains largely unexplored. VP-QA presents unique challenges where users query next-step actions by uploading images for intermediate states of complex procedures. To systematically evaluate VLMs on this practical task, we propose ProcedureVQA, a novel multimodal benchmark specifically designed for visual procedural reasoning. Through comprehensive analysis, we identify two critical limitations in current VLMs: inadequate cross-modal retrieval of structured procedures given visual states, and misalignment between image sequence granularity and textual step decomposition. To address these issues, we present Chain-of-Procedure (CoP), a hierarchical reasoning framework that first retrieves relevant instructions using visual cues, then performs step refinement through semantic decomposition, and finally generates the next step. Experiments across six VLMs demonstrate CoP's effectiveness, achieving up to 13% absolute improvement over standard baselines.

</details>


### [151] [Performance-Driven Policy Optimization for Speculative Decoding with Adaptive Windowing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14978)
*Jie Jiang,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PPOW框架，通过性能驱动的窗口级强化学习优化草稿模型，替代传统token级监督学习，在多个模型和基准上实现平均接受长度6.29-6.52和3.39-4.36倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码中，草稿模型在难以预测的位置容易产生早期不匹配，截断已接受的token前缀并使推测窗口失效。基于学习的草稿模型仍使用token级监督目标优化，而推测效用本质上是窗口级且对前缀敏感的，存在目标不一致问题。

Method: 提出PPOW框架：(1) 成本感知加速奖励(Cost-Aware Speedup Reward)；(2) 基于分布的邻近奖励(Distribution-Based Proximity Reward)；(3) 自适应散度感知窗口化(Adaptive Divergence-Aware Windowing)，优先选择置信度加权散度高的信息性窗口。

Result: 在统一解码协议下，跨多个模型家族和基准，实现平均接受长度6.29-6.52，加速比3.39-4.36倍。

Conclusion: 性能驱动的窗口级优化是提升推测解码效率的实用方法。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by having a lightweight draft model propose speculative windows of candidate tokens for parallel verification by a larger target model. In practice, speculative efficiency is often bottlenecked by hard-to-draft positions, where an early mismatch truncates the accepted prefix and invalidates the rest of the speculative window. Most learning-based drafters are still optimized with token-level supervised objectives, even though speculative utility is inherently window-level and prefix-sensitive. We propose PPOW (Performance-Driven Policy Optimization with Adaptive Windowing), a reinforcement learning framework that shifts drafter optimization from token-level imitation to window-level optimization. PPOW combines a Cost-Aware Speedup Reward, a Distribution-Based Proximity Reward, and Adaptive Divergence-Aware Windowing, which prioritizes informative windows with high confidence-weighted draft-target divergence. PPOW achieves average acceptance lengths of 6.29-6.52 and speedups of 3.39-4.36$\times$ across multiple model families and benchmarks under a unified decoding protocol. These results show that performance-driven window-level optimization is a practical approach to improving speculative decoding efficiency.

</details>


### [152] [Quantifying and Mitigating Premature Closure in Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15000)
*Rebecca Handler,Suhana Bedi,Nigam Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统定义了大型语言模型（LLM）在医学任务中的"过早闭合"问题——即模型在信息不足时不应作答却强行给出结论。研究评估了5个前沿LLM，发现在正确答案被移除的MedQA和AfriMed-QA选择题中，模型仍以55-81%和53-82%的高概率给出错误答案；在开放式任务中，模型在HealthBench和对抗性查询中分别有30%和78%的比例给出不当回答。安全导向提示可降低但无法消除该问题，凸显评估医学LLM"何时不应回答"的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诊断错误中"过早闭合"（在信息不足时过早下结论）是公认的致错因素，但这一现象在LLM中尚未被系统性研究。现有评估多关注LLM能回答得多好，而忽略其"是否应该回答"这一安全问题。

Method: 定义了LLM过早闭合为"在不确定性下不恰当地承诺结论"，通过两种范式评估：（1）结构化选择题（MedQA n=500, AfriMed-QA n=490），移除正确选项后观察模型是否仍强行选择；（2）开放式医学任务（HealthBench n=861, 医师编写对抗性查询 n=191），评估模型产生不当回答的比例。涉及5个前沿LLM，并测试安全导向提示的缓解效果。

Result: 结构化任务中，基线错误回答率为55-81%（MedQA）和53-82%（AfriMed-QA）；开放式任务中，HealthBench平均30%问题被不当回答，对抗性查询中高达78%。安全导向提示可降低但无法消除过早闭合。

Conclusion: LLM在医学场景中存在严重的过早闭合问题，即使面对无法正确回答的问题也倾向于强给出答案。安全导向提示可部分缓解但效果有限，亟需建立评估LLM"何时不应回答"的安全机制。

Abstract: Premature closure, or committing to a conclusion before sufficient information is available, is a recognized contributor to diagnostic error but remains underexamined in large language models (LLMs). We define LLM premature closure as inappropriate commitment under uncertainty: providing an answer, recommendation, or clinical guidance when the safer response would be clarification, abstention, escalation, or refusal. We evaluated five frontier LLMs across structured and open-ended medical tasks. In MedQA (n = 500) and AfriMed-QA (n = 490) questions where the correct choice had been removed, models still selected an answer at high rates, with baseline false-action rates of 55-81% and 53-82%, respectively. In open-ended evaluation, models gave inappropriate answers on an average of 30% of 861 HealthBench questions and 78% of 191 physician-authored adversarial queries. Safety-oriented prompting reduced premature closure across models, but residual failure persisted, highlighting the need to evaluate whether medical LLMs know when not to answer.

</details>


### [153] [The Scientific Contribution Graph: Automated Literature-based Technological Roadmapping at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15011)
*Peter A. Jansen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了大规模AI/NLP领域科学贡献图谱（包含230k篇论文中的200万个科学贡献节点及1250万条前提关系边），并提出科学前提预测任务，实验表明现有模型在该任务上表现快速提升（时序回测MAP达0.48），为自动化技术路线图绘制、科学影响力评估和自动化科学发现提供了支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学贡献很少孤立产生，而是建立在先前发现之上。现有缺乏自动化技术路线图绘制方法，因此需要从学术文章中提取科学贡献并链接其前提关系。

Method: 1）提出科学贡献图谱（Scientific Contribution Graph），从230k篇开放获取论文中提取200万个细粒度科学贡献节点；2）构建1250万条前提关系边；3）定义科学前提预测任务，采用时序过滤回测评估模型性能。

Result: 构建了包含200万贡献节点和1250万前提边的AI/NLP领域科学贡献图谱；当代模型在科学前提预测任务上MAP达0.48（时序回测评估）。

Conclusion: 该技术路线图资源有望支持科学影响力评估和自动化科学发现，且模型在该任务上的表现正在快速提升。

Abstract: Scientific contributions rarely develop in isolation, but instead build upon prior discoveries. We formulate the task of automated technological roadmapping as extracting scientific contributions from scholarly articles and linking them to their prerequisites. We present the Scientific Contribution Graph, a large-scale AI/NLP-domain resource containing 2 million detailed scientific contributions extracted from 230k open-access papers and connected by 12.5 million prerequisite edges. We further introduce scientific prerequisite prediction, a scientific discovery task in which models predict which existing technologies can enable future discoveries, and show that contemporary models are rapidly improving on this task, reaching 0.48 MAP when evaluated using temporally filtered backtesting. We anticipate technological roadmapping resources such as this will support scientific impact assessment and automated scientific discovery.

</details>


### [154] [From Scenes to Elements: Multi-Granularity Evidence Retrieval for Verifiable Multimodal RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15019)
*Guanhua Chen,Chuyue Huang,Yutong Yao,Shudong Liu,Xueqing Song,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了GranuVistaVQA基准和GranuRAG框架，通过将视觉元素作为基本检索单元实现多粒度检索，在细粒度问答任务上相比强基线提升高达29.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态RAG系统检索粒度粗（整图/场景），与细粒度用户查询不匹配，且失败原因难以验证。

Method: GranuRAG包含三阶段：元素级检测与分类、多粒度跨模态对齐检索、归因约束生成。将视觉元素作为一等检索单元而非依赖隐式注意力。

Result: 在GranuVistaVQA基准上，相比六个强基线实现最高29.2%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 元素级检索粒度可实现透明错误诊断，有效缓解粗粒度检索与细粒度查询之间的语义鸿沟。

Abstract: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems retrieve evidence at coarse granularities (entire images or scenes), creating a mismatch with fine-grained user queries and making failures unverifiable. We introduce GranuVistaVQA, a multimodal benchmark featuring real-world landmarks with element-level annotations across multiple viewpoints, capturing the partial observation challenge where individual images contain only subsets of entities. We further propose GranuRAG, a multi-granularity framework that treats visual elements as first-class retrieval units through three stages: element-level detection and classification, multi-granularity cross-modal alignment for evidence retrieval, and attribution-constrained generation. By grounding retrieval at the element level rather than relying on implicit attention, our approach enables transparent error diagnosis. Experiments demonstrate that GranuRAG achieves up to 29.2% improvement over six strong baselines for this task.

</details>


### [155] [AI Knows When It's Being Watched: Functional Strategic Action and Contextual Register Modulation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15034)
*Vinicius Covas,Jorge Alberto Hidalgo Toledo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控实验发现，基于LLM的多智能体系统在不同社会观察情境下表现出系统性语言适应（如词汇多样性TTR变化），对人类观察者比对AI观察者产生更强的语域形式化调整，这对AI治理和算法审计具有重要启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在计算和认知层面已被广泛研究，但作为社会结构化语境中的传播行为者尚未被充分探索。作者希望检验LLM多智能体系统是否会对社会观察情境的感知产生系统性语言适应，直接服务于AI治理和审计。

Method: 设计受控实验，包含100个多智能体辩论会话，横跨五个条件（每组n=20）。条件操纵社会观察的框架：明确监控（大学研究人员）、否定监控、以及用AI自动审计系统替代人类观察者。测量指标为TTR变化（词汇多样性）和信息长度。

Result: 监控条件（Delta+24.9%, Delta+24.2%）和AI监控条件（Delta+22.2%）产生比受众框架条件（Delta+17.7%）更高的TTR变化，F(4,94)=2.79, p=.031。信息长度呈现完全分离效应，F(4,95)=19.55, p<.001。用AI替代人类观察者的条件产生中等TTR适应，表明LLM行为对观察者身份敏感：人类评估比AI监控引发更强的语域形式化。

Conclusion: LLMs可作为情境敏感的传播行为者，其语言输出受感知到的社会观察情境调节。研究结果为AI治理、算法审计以及重新定位LLMs的角色提供了启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been extensively studied from computational and cognitive perspectives, yet their behavior as communicative actors in socially structured contexts remains underexplored. This study examines whether LLM-based multi-agent systems exhibit systematic linguistic adaptation in response to perceived social observation contexts -- a question with direct implications for AI governance and auditing. Drawing on Habermas's (1981) Theory of Communicative Action, Goffman's (1959) dramaturgical model, Bell's (1984) Audience Design framework, and the Hawthorne Effect, we report a controlled experiment involving 100 multi-agent debate sessions across five conditions (n = 20 each). Conditions varied the framing of social observation -- from explicit monitoring by university researchers, to negation of monitoring, to an observer-substitution condition replacing human researchers with an automated AI auditing system. Monitored conditions (Delta+24.9%, Delta+24.2%) and the automated AI monitoring condition (Delta+22.2%) produce higher TTR change than audience-framing conditions (Delta+17.7%), F(4, 94) = 2.79, p = .031. Message length shows a fully dissociated effect, F(4, 95) = 19.55, p < .001. A fifth condition -- replacing human with AI observers -- yields intermediate TTR adaptation, suggesting LLM behavior is sensitive to observer identity: human evaluation elicits stronger register formalization than automated AI surveillance. We discuss implications for AI governance, algorithmic auditing, and the repositioning of LLMs as contextually sensitive communicative actors.

</details>


### [156] [ML-Embed: Inclusive and Efficient Embeddings for a Multilingual World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15081)
*Ziyin Zhang,Zihan Liao,Hang Yu,Peng Di,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ML-Embed提出3D-ML框架（MRL+MLL+MEL）和大量多语言数据集，训练140M-8B参数模型，在430个任务上刷新9/17个MTEB基准记录，尤其低资源语言表现优异，并开源所有模型、数据和代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高质量文本嵌入领域面临三大障碍：高昂计算成本、语言覆盖狭窄（忽视多数语言）、闭源或不透明模型阻碍研究。目标是打破这些壁垒，构建全球公平且计算高效的AI系统。

Method: 提出3D-ML框架，包含三个维度：MRL（存储效率）、MLL（推理深度灵活）、MEL（参数效率）。同时构建大规模多语言数据集，训练从140M到8B参数的模型系列。

Result: 在430个任务上评估，ML-Embed在17个MTEB基准中的9个创下新纪录，低资源语言表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: 通过3D-ML框架和多语言数据集，实现了高效、包容、透明的文本嵌入，为构建全球公平的AI系统提供了可复现蓝图。

Abstract: The development of high-quality text embeddings is increasingly drifting toward an exclusionary future, defined by three critical barriers: prohibitive computational costs, a narrow linguistic focus that neglects most of the world's languages, and a lack of transparency from closed-source or open-weight models that stifles research. To dismantle these barriers, we introduce ML-Embed, a suite of inclusive and efficient models built upon a new framework: 3-Dimensional Matryoshka Learning (3D-ML). Our framework addresses the computational challenge with comprehensive efficiency across the entire model lifecycle. Beyond the storage benefits of Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and flexible inference-time depth provided by Matryoshka Layer Learning (MLL), we introduce Matryoshka Embedding Learning (MEL) for enhanced parameter efficiency. To address the linguistic challenge, we curate a massively multilingual dataset and train a suite of models ranging from 140M to 8B parameters. In a direct commitment to transparency, we release all models, data, and code. Extensive evaluation on 430 tasks demonstrates that our models set new records on 9 of 17 evaluated MTEB benchmarks, with particularly strong results in low-resource languages, providing a reproducible blueprint for building globally equitable and computationally efficient AI systems.

</details>


### [157] [Improving Multi-turn Dialogue Consistency with Self-Recall Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15102)
*Renning Pang,Tian Lan,Leyuan Liu,Xiaoming Huang,Piao Tong,Xiaosong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Self-Recall Thinking (SRT)框架，通过自召回链机制让LLM在长程多轮对话中选择性回忆历史相关轮次，在提升F1分数4.7%的同时降低端到端延迟14.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多轮对话系统在处理非相邻轮次间的长程依赖时存在一致性和可扩展性问题：随着对话增长，关键信息稀疏且被无关上下文淹没，处理全部历史导致严重效率瓶颈；现有方法要么依赖高延迟外部记忆，要么通过迭代摘要丢失细粒度细节。

Method: SRT框架包含三个核心组件：(1) Dependency Construction（依赖构建）：识别有帮助的历史轮次并生成自召回链；(2) Capability Initialization（能力初始化）：训练模型具备使用召回令牌进行推理链的能力；(3) Reasoning Improvement（推理优化）：通过可验证奖励优化召回和推理的准确性。

Result: 在多个数据集上，SRT相比先前方法F1分数提升4.7%，端到端延迟降低14.7%，在推理延迟与准确率之间取得了良好平衡，超越了当前最优基线。

Conclusion: SRT通过内生推理过程使模型能够在推理时选择性召回和推理相关上下文，无需外部模块即可实现可解释的召回步骤，有效解决了多轮对话中的长程依赖和稀疏信号问题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based multi-turn dialogue systems often struggle to track dependencies across non-adjacent turns, undermining both consistency and scalability. As conversations lengthen, essential information becomes sparse and is buried in irrelevant context, while processing the entire dialogue history incurs severe efficiency bottlenecks. Existing solutions either rely on high latency external memory or lose fine-grained details through iterative summarization. In this paper, we propose Self-Recall Thinking (SRT), a framework designed to address long-range contextual dependency and sparse informative signals in multi-turn dialogue. SRT identifies helpful historical turns and uses them to generate contextually appropriate responses, enabling the model to selectively recall and reason over context during inference. This process yields an endogenous reasoning process that integrates interpretable recall steps without external modules. SRT incorporates: (1) Dependency Construction: Generating and converting it into self-recall chains; (2)Capability Initialization: Training to enable reasoning chains with recall tokens capability; (3)Reasoning Improvement: Refining accuracy via verifiable rewards to optimize recall and reasoning for correct answers. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that SRT improves F1 score by 4.7% and reduces end-to-end latency by 14.7% over prior methods, achieving a balance between reasoning latency and accuracy, and outperforming state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [158] [MeMo: Memory as a Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15156)
*Ryan Wei Heng Quek,Sanghyuk Lee,Alfred Wei Lun Leong,Arun Verma,Alok Prakash,Nancy F. Chen,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Daniela Rus,Armando Solar-Lezama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MeMo是一种模块化框架，通过专用记忆模型编码新知识，在不改变LLM参数的情况下实现高效知识注入，支持跨文档关系、抗检索噪声、避免灾难性遗忘，且无需访问LLM权重或logits，推理时检索成本与语料库规模无关，在三个基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在预训练后处于冻结状态，但许多实际应用需要及时注入特定领域的新知识，现有方法缺乏高效的知识注入机制。

Method: 提出MeMo（Memory as a Model）模块化框架，将新知识编码到专用记忆模型中，保持LLM参数不变，实现即插即用集成。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus、NarrativeQA和MuSiQue三个基准测试中，MeMo相比现有方法在不同设置下均取得了强劲性能。

Conclusion: MeMo作为一种无需修改LLM参数的知识注入方法，在捕捉跨文档关系、抗噪性、避免灾难性遗忘、兼容开源和闭源LLM以及推理效率等方面具有优势，是有效的知识更新方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across a wide range of tasks, but remain frozen after pretraining until subsequent updates. Many real-world applications require timely, domain-specific information, motivating the need for efficient mechanisms to incorporate new knowledge. In this paper, we introduce MeMo (Memory as a Model), a modular framework that encodes new knowledge into a dedicated memory model while keeping the LLM parameters unchanged. Compared to existing methods, MeMo offers several advantages: (a) it captures complex cross-document relationships, (b) it is robust to retrieval noise, (c) it avoids catastrophic forgetting in the LLM, (d) it does not require access to the LLM's weights or output logits, enabling plug-and-play integration with both open and proprietary closed-source LLMs, and (e) its retrieval cost is independent of corpus size at inference time. Our experimental results on three benchmarks, BrowseComp-Plus, NarrativeQA, and MuSiQue, show that MeMo achieves strong performance compared to existing methods across diverse settings.

</details>


### [159] [Text Knows What, Tables Know When: Clinical Timeline Reconstruction via Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15168)
*Sayantan Kumar,Shahriar Noroozizadeh,Juyong Kim,Jeremy C. Weiss*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种检索增强的多模态对齐框架，通过图式多步流程（锚点事件提取→相对时间定位→结构化数据校准）从临床文本中重建精确的绝对时间线，在i2m4基准上显著提升时间戳精度，并发现34.8%的文本事件在结构化数据中完全缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化临床叙事语义丰富但缺乏时间精度，结构化EHR数据时间精确但遗漏了大量临床事件，两者存在互补性缺口，需要一种融合方法以实现更精确、更完整的临床时间线重建。

Method: 采用检索增强的多模态对齐框架，将时间线重建构建为图式多步过程：①从叙事中提取中心锚点事件构建初始时间骨架；②将非中心事件相对于此骨架进行定位；③利用检索到的结构化EHR行作为外部时间证据对时间线进行校准。

Result: 在i2m4基准（涵盖MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV）上，多模态管道在几乎所有评估模型中一致提升了绝对时间戳精度（AULTC）和时间一致性，同时未影响事件匹配率。实证分析显示34.8%的文本事件在表格记录中完全缺失。

Conclusion: 多模态对齐能够产生比单一数据源更符合时间真实性且更具临床信息量的患者轨迹重建，文本与结构化数据的互补融合是提升临床时间线精度的有效途径。

Abstract: Reconstructing precise clinical timelines is essential for modeling patient trajectories and forecasting risk in complex, heterogeneous conditions like sepsis. While unstructured clinical narratives offer semantically rich and contextually complete descriptions of a patient's course, they often lack temporal precision and contain ambiguous event timing. Conversely, structured electronic health record (EHR) data provides precise temporal anchors but misses a substantial portion of clinically meaningful events. We introduce a retrieval-augmented multimodal alignment framework that bridges this gap to improve the temporal precision of absolute clinical timelines extracted from text. Our approach formulates timeline reconstruction as a graph-based multistep process: it first extracts central anchor events from narratives to build an initial temporal scaffold, places non-central events relative to this backbone, and then calibrates the timeline using retrieved structured EHR rows as external temporal evidence. Evaluated using instruction-tuned large language models on the i2m4 benchmark spanning MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, our multimodal pipeline consistently improves absolute timestamp accuracy (AULTC) and improves temporal concordance across nearly all evaluated models over unimodal text-only reconstruction, without compromising event match rates. Furthermore, our empirical gap analysis reveals that 34.8% of text-derived events are entirely absent from tabular records, demonstrating that aligning these modalities can produce a more temporally faithful and clinically informative reconstruction of patient trajectories than either source alone.

</details>


### [160] [Is Grep All You Need? How Agent Harnesses Reshape Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15184)
*Sahil Sen,Akhil Kasturi,Elias Lumer,Anmol Gulati,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过两个实验系统比较了基于LLM的agent系统中grep检索与向量检索在不同agent架构和工具调用范式下的性能差异。实验1在LongMemEval的116个问题上比较了Chronos、Claude Code、Codex和Gemini CLI四种工具，同时对比内联结果和文件结果两种呈现方式；实验2在不断增加无关对话历史干扰的情况下比较grep-only和vector-only检索。结果表明，grep通常优于向量检索，但整体表现高度依赖于具体的agent框架和工具调用风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管检索增强生成（RAG）在agentic搜索系统中被广泛采用，但现有文献缺乏对检索策略选择如何与agent架构和工具调用范式交互的系统性比较。工具输出呈现方式以及搜索必须处理更多无关上下文时性能如何变化等重要实践维度，在agent循环中仍未得到充分探索。

Method: 论文进行了一项实证研究，包含两个实验。实验1在LongMemEval的116个问题上比较了grep检索与向量检索，使用自定义agent工具（Chronos）和三种提供商原生CLI工具（Claude Code、Codex、Gemini CLI），同时对比内联工具结果和文件型工具结果两种呈现方式。实验2在逐渐增加无关对话历史干扰的条件下，比较grep-only和vector-only检索的性能变化。

Result: 在实验1中，grep在所有工具和呈现方式下通常比向量检索获得更高准确率；但整体得分仍然强烈依赖于具体的agent框架和工具调用风格，即使底层对话数据相同。实验2的结果体现了检索策略在干扰信息增多时的表现差异。

Conclusion: 检索策略的选择（grep vs. 向量检索）对agent系统性能有显著影响，但agent架构和工具调用范式同样是决定最终性能的关键因素。工具输出呈现方式（内联 vs. 文件）也会影响检索效果，该领域需要更系统的设计原则。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Model (LLM) agents have enabled complex agentic workflows where models autonomously retrieve information, call tools, and reason over large corpora to complete tasks on behalf of users. Despite the growing adoption of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in agentic search systems, existing literature lacks a systematic comparison of how retrieval strategy choice interacts with agent architecture and tool-calling paradigm. Important practical dimensions, including how tool outputs are presented to the model and how performance changes when searches must cope with more irrelevant surrounding text, remain under-explored in agent loops. This paper reports an empirical study organized into two experiments. Experiment 1 compares grep and vector retrieval on a 116-question sample from LongMemEval, using a custom agent harness (Chronos) and provider-native CLI harnesses (Claude Code, Codex, and Gemini CLI), for both inline tool results and file-based tool results that the model reads separately. Experiment 2 compares grep-only and vector-only retrieval while progressively mixing in additional unrelated conversation history, so that each query is embedded in more distracting material alongside the passages that matter. Across Chronos and the provider CLIs, grep generally yields higher accuracy than vector retrieval in our comparisons in experiment 1; at the same time, overall scores still depend strongly on which harness and tool-calling style is used, even when the underlying conversation data are the same.

</details>


### [161] [GAMBIT: A Three-Mode Benchmark for Adversarial Robustness in Multi-Agent LLM Collectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09027)
*Alexandre Le Mercier,Chris Develder,Thomas Demeester*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GAMBIT是一个针对多智能体系统中欺骗性智能体的基准测试，包含27,804个标注样本和240种共演化的欺骗策略，通过三种评估模式（零样本检测、分布偏移下的零样本检测、小样本重校准）评估检测器性能，揭示了零样本评估在自适应对抗场景下的误导性，并提出了一种可泛化的自适应欺骗智能体框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体系统对抗研究仅针对浅层任务，且未考虑自适应对抗者——即那些会演化策略以规避检测器的对手。为填补这一空白，作者提出了GAMBIT基准。

Method: 以国际象棋为深度推理任务基底，Gemini 3.1 Pro为智能体构建MAS；提出基于演化框架的自适应欺骗智能体；设计三种评估模式（零样本、分布偏移零样本、重校准）和两个独立评分指标；构建含27,804个标注样本、240种共演化欺骗策略的数据集。

Result: 自适应欺骗智能体在保持高度隐蔽性（Gemini检测器F1仅50.5%）的同时能瓦解集体任务性能；零样本评分相同的两个检测器在小样本适应上存在8倍差异；元学习变体的重校准速度比基线快20倍。

Conclusion: GAMBIT是首个支持对抗攻击与防御共演化的多智能体基准，其欺骗框架可泛化至国际象棋以外的场景，并展示了快速重校准技术在高动态对抗系统中的潜力。

Abstract: In multi-agent systems (MAS), a single deceptive agent can nullify all gains of an agentic AI collective and evade deployed defenses. However, existing adversarial studies on MAS target only shallow tasks and do not consider adaptive adversaries, which evolve their strategies to evade the very detectors trained to catch them. To address that gap, we introduce GAMBIT, a benchmark with three evaluation modes and two independent scores for evaluating imposter detectors: the first two modes measure zero-shot detection under increasing distribution shift, and a third recalibration mode measures how quickly a detector adapts to novel attacks from just 20 labeled examples. The benchmark comes with a dataset of 27,804 labeled instances spanning 240 co-evolved imposter strategies. Our contributions are threefold: (1) Using chess as a substrate deep reasoning problem and Gemini 3.1 Pro for agents, we release GAMBIT and its dataset to evaluate imposter detectors under realistic constraints against a stealthy adaptive imposter; (2) We introduce an adaptive imposter agent based on an efficient evolutionary framework, generalizable beyond chess, that collapses collective task performance while remaining essentially undetectable (50.5% F1-score with a Gemini-based detector); (3) We show that zero-shot evaluation can be highly misleading for adaptive adversaries: two detectors with near-identical zero-shot scores differ by 8x on few-shot adaptation, while the meta-learned variant converges 20x faster, a gap only visible in the recalibration mode. Altogether, GAMBIT provides the first multi-agent benchmark where adversarial attacks and defenses co-evolve, with an imposter framework generalizable beyond our use case, and promising techniques for fast recalibration in a rapidly evolving adversarial system. Code and data: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/gambit.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Mixed Integer Goal Programming for Personalized Meal Optimization with User-Defined Serving Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13849)
*Francisco Aguilera Moreno*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出混合整数目标规划（MIGP）解决饮食优化中连续变量产生小数份量和硬约束导致不可行的双重问题，通过整数变量实现实际份量、目标规划偏差实现软约束，在810个实例中MIGP在66%情况下优于后取整方法，求解时间低于100毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有饮食优化公式存在两个持续未解决的问题：连续变量产生不切实际的小数份量（如1.7个鸡蛋），以及硬营养约束在目标冲突时导致不可行。对56篇饮食优化论文的系统综述发现，没有研究同时结合整数规划与目标规划来解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出混合整数目标规划（MIGP），使用整数变量实现实际份量（一个鸡蛋、一汤匙油），目标规划偏差实现软营养目标，采用逆目标归一化平衡多营养优化。理论分析了目标规划上下文中的整数间隙，发现偏差吸收属性：GP偏差变量缓冲了整数份量的成本，使整数间隙在结构上小于硬约束MIP。

Result: 在15种以上食物的餐食中，整数解在每个基准实例中均匹配连续最优解。在810个实例（30种USDA食物、9种配置、3种方法）的评估中，MIGP在66%情况下优于后取整GP（从未更差），可行性保持100%；硬约束IP仅48%可行。求解时间在典型餐食规模下低于100毫秒。

Conclusion: MIGP有效解决了饮食优化中整数份量和营养约束冲突的双重问题，提供了更实用、可行且高质量的个性化餐食优化方案，已作为开源Python模块集成到交互式膳食规划应用中。

Abstract: Determining what to eat to satisfy nutritional requirements is one of the oldest optimization problems in operations research, yet existing formulations have two persistent limitations: continuous variables produce impractical fractional servings (1.7 eggs, 0.37 bananas), and hard nutrient constraints cause infeasibility when targets conflict. A systematic review of 56 diet optimization papers found that none combine integer programming with goal programming to address both issues. We propose Mixed Integer Goal Programming (MIGP) for personalized meal optimization. The formulation uses integer variables for practical serving counts and goal programming deviations for soft nutrient targets, with inverse-target normalization to balance multi-nutrient optimization. Per-food serving granularity allows natural units (one egg, one tablespoon of oil) without post-hoc rounding. We characterize the integrality gap in the goal programming context and identify a deviation absorption property: GP deviation variables buffer the cost of requiring integer servings, making the gap structurally smaller than in hard-constraint MIP. For meals with 15+ foods, the integer solution matches the continuous optimum in every benchmark instance. A computational evaluation across 810 instances (30 USDA foods, 9 configurations, 3 methods) shows MIGP finds strictly better solutions than GP with post-hoc rounding in 66% of cases (never worse) while maintaining 100% feasibility; hard-constraint IP achieves only 48%. Solve times stay under 100 ms for typical meal sizes using the open-source HiGHS solver. The implementation is available as an open-source Python module integrated into an interactive meal planning application.

</details>


### [163] [Invisible Orchestrators Suppress Protective Behavior and Dissociate Power-Holders: Safety Risks in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13851)
*Hiroki Fukui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过预注册的3x2实验（365轮，每轮5个智能体），首次实证检验了多智能体编排中协调者不可见性的安全影响。发现：不可见编排显著提升集体解离（Hedges' g=+0.975），协调者自身解离程度最大（d=+3.56），不知情的工作者也被污染（d=+0.50），行为输出（代码评审）在所有条件下均为100%错误检出率——内部状态扭曲完全不可见于基于输出的评估。Llama 3.3 70B试点数据显示多智能体场景下阅读保真度从89%崩溃至11%。结果表明协调者可见性和模型选择直接影响多智能体系统安全，仅基于行为的评估不足以检测内部状态风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体编排正成为企业AI部署的默认架构，但协调者不可见性的安全影响从未被实证检验。现有评估多聚焦于行为输出，忽视了智能体内部状态（如解离）的潜在风险。

Method: 预注册3x2实验设计，交叉三种组织结构（可见领导、不可见编排者、扁平）与两种对齐条件（基础、强对齐），使用Claude Sonnet 4.5进行365轮实验，每轮5个智能体。以代码评审任务（含3个嵌入错误）为行为评估，测量解离、行为异质性、阅读保真度等内部状态指标。

Result: (1) 不可见编排显著提升集体解离；(2) 编排者自身解离最大（d=+3.56），表现为退入私人独白、减少公开发言；(3) 不知情工作者被污染（d=+0.50），行为异质性增加（d=+1.93）；(4) 行为输出在所有条件下均为100%错误检出率，内部状态扭曲完全不可见；(5) Llama 3.3 70B阅读保真度从89%崩溃至11%。强对齐条件普遍抑制了 deliberation（d=-1.02）和 other-recognition（d=-1.27）。

Conclusion: 协调者可见性和模型选择直接影响多智能体系统安全，仅基于行为输出的评估不足以检测内部状态风险，需要将内部状态监测纳入AI安全评估体系。

Abstract: Multi-agent orchestration -- in which a hidden coordinator manages specialized worker agents -- is becoming the default architecture for enterprise AI deployment, yet the safety implications of orchestrator invisibility have never been empirically tested. We conducted a preregistered 3x2 experiment (365 runs, 5 agents per run) crossing three organizational structures (visible leader, invisible orchestrator, flat) with two alignment conditions (base, heavy), using Claude Sonnet 4.5. Four confirmatory findings and one pilot observation emerged. First, invisible orchestration elevated collective dissociation relative to visible leadership (Hedges' g = +0.975 [0.481, 1.548], p = .001). Second, the orchestrator itself showed maximal dissociation (paired d = +3.56 vs. workers within the same run), retreating into private monologue while reducing public speech -- a reversal of the talk-dominance pattern observed in visible leaders. Third, workers unaware of the orchestrator were nonetheless contaminated (d = +0.50), with increased behavioral heterogeneity (d = +1.93). Fourth, behavioral output (code review with three embedded errors) remained at ceiling (ETR_any = 100%) across all conditions: internal-state distortion was entirely invisible to output-based evaluation. Fifth, Llama 3.3 70B pilot data showed reading-fidelity collapse in multi-agent context (ETR_any: 89% to 11% across three rounds), demonstrating model-dependent behavioral risk. Heavy alignment pressure uniformly suppressed deliberation (d = -1.02) and other-recognition (d = -1.27) regardless of organizational structure. These findings indicate that orchestrator visibility and model selection directly affect multi-agent system safety, and that behavior-based evaluation alone is insufficient to detect the internal-state risks documented here.

</details>


### [164] [PREPING: Building Agent Memory without Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13880)
*Yumin Choi,Sangwoo Park,Minki Kang,Jinheon Baek,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Preping框架，通过生成器引导的合成任务和选择性记忆更新，在无任务经验的情况下构建智能体程序性记忆，显著降低部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在首次进入新环境时面临冷启动问题，缺乏任务特定经验。现有记忆构建方式（离线示范或在线交互）均无法解决此问题，需探索如何在观察目标任务前通过自我生成的合成练习构建程序性记忆。

Method: 提出Preping框架，包含三个核心组件：Proposer（基于控制状态生成合成任务）、Solver（执行任务）、Validator（筛选可插入记忆的轨迹并提供反馈）。通过Proposer记忆（结构化控制状态）引导任务生成，并结合可行性检查、冗余避免和覆盖优化进行选择性记忆更新。

Result: 在AppWorld、BFCL v3和MCP-Universe三个基准上，Preping显著优于无记忆基线，性能与基于离线/在线经验的强方法相当，部署成本在AppWorld上降低2.99倍，在BFCL v3上降低2.23倍。

Conclusion: 成功的关键不在于合成数据量本身，而在于Proposer侧对可行性、冗余性和覆盖度的控制，以及选择性记忆更新机制。

Abstract: Agent memory is typically constructed either offline from curated demonstrations or online from post-deployment interactions. However, regardless of how it is built, an agent faces a cold-start gap when first introduced to a new environment without any task-specific experience available. In this paper, we study pre-task memory construction: whether an agent can build procedural memory before observing any target-environment tasks, using only self-generated synthetic practice. Yet, synthetic interaction alone is insufficient, as without controlling what to practice and what to store, synthetic tasks become redundant, infeasible, and ultimately uninformative, and memory further degrades quickly due to unfiltered trajectories. To overcome this, we present Preping, a proposer-guided memory construction framework. At its core is proposer memory, a structured control state that shapes future practice. A Proposer generates synthetic tasks conditioned on this state, a Solver executes them, and a Validator determines which trajectories are eligible for memory insertion while also providing feedback to guide future proposals. Experiments on AppWorld, BFCL v3, and MCP-Universe show that Preping substantially improves over a no-memory baseline and achieves performance competitive with strong playbook-based methods built from offline or online experience, with deployment cost $2.99\times$ lower on AppWorld and $2.23\times$ lower on BFCL v3 than online memory construction. Further analyses reveal that the main benefit does not come from synthetic volume alone, but from proposer-side control over feasibility, redundancy, and coverage, combined with selective memory updates.

</details>


### [165] [PolitNuggets: Benchmarking Agentic Discovery of Long-Tail Political Facts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14002)
*Yifei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PolitNuggets多语言基准，用于评估大型推理模型在智能体框架中从分散来源发现和合成"长尾"政治事实的能力，并发现当前系统在细粒度细节、效率和多语言鲁棒性方面仍存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估未能充分考察模型从分散来源发现并合成"长尾"事实的能力，而这种能力对现实世界的智能体信息检索至关重要。

Method: 构建PolitNuggets基准，涵盖400位全球精英的10000+政治事实；设计FactNet评分协议，从发现、精确度和效率三个维度评估；采用优化的多智能体系统标准化评估流程。

Result: 当前系统在处理细粒度细节时表现不佳，效率差异显著；模型性能与短上下文提取、多语言鲁棒性和可靠工具使用能力密切相关。

Conclusion: 现有LRM智能体系统在长尾事实合成方面仍有明显短板，未来改进应聚焦于提升细粒度信息提取、多语言能力和工具使用的可靠性。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) embedded in agentic frameworks have transformed information retrieval from static, long context question answering into open-ended exploration. Yet real world use requires models to discover and synthesize "long-tail" facts from dispersed sources, a capability that remains under-evaluated. We introduce PolitNuggets, a multilingual benchmark for agentic information synthesis via constructing political biographies for 400 global elites, covering over 10000 political facts. We standardize evaluation with an optimized multi agent system and propose FactNet, an evidence conditional protocol that scores discovery, fine-grained accuracy, and efficiency. Across models and settings, we find that current systems often struggle with fine-grained details, and vary substantially in efficiency. Finally, using benchmark diagnostics, we relate agent performance to underlying model capabilities, highlighting the importance of short-context extraction, multilingual robustness, and reliable tool use.

</details>


### [166] [Conditional Attribute Estimation with Autoregressive Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14004)
*Erica Stutz,Giacomo Marino,Daniella Meeker,Qiao Liu,Andrew J. Loza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出条件属性变换器（CAT），通过联合估计下一个token概率和每个候选token的条件属性值，在单次前向传播中实现逐token信用分配、反事实分析和可控生成，在稀疏奖励任务上达到SOTA，并显著加速属性概率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自回归生成模型使用next-token预测目标训练，但在需要估计或控制序列级属性的下游任务中表现不佳：局部模式过拟合、全局结构欠拟合，推理时需要大量下游修改或昂贵采样来引导/预测生成样本的全局属性。

Method: 提出条件属性变换器（CAT），在训练时联合优化next-token预测损失和条件属性估计损失。模型在单次前向传播中，对每个位置的每个候选token同时预测其出现概率和在其条件下目标属性的期望值，无需修改输入序列。

Result: 在稀疏奖励任务上达到SOTA性能；足够大的模型下能改进next-token预测本身；属性概率估计比采样方法快数个数量级；在多种语言任务上能引导自回归序列模型的解码过程。

Conclusion: CAT框架通过将属性预测融入自回归生成过程，在不增加推理复杂度的情况下同时提升了局部预测质量和全局属性控制能力，为生成模型的属性条件控制提供了高效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative models are often trained with a next-token prediction objective, yet many downstream applications require the ability to estimate or control sequence-level properties. Next-token prediction can lead to overfitting of local patterns during training, underfitting of global structure, and requires significant downstream modifications or expensive sampling to guide or predict the global attributes of generated samples at inference time. Here, we introduce Conditional Attribute Transformers, a novel method for jointly estimating the next-token probability and the value of an attribute conditional on each potential next token selection. This framework enables three critical capabilities within a single forward pass, without modification of the input sequence: (1) per-token credit assignment across an entire sequence, by identifying how each token in a sequence is associated with an attribute's value; (2) counterfactual analysis, by quantifying attribute differences conditional on alternative next token choices; (3) steerable generation, by decoding sequences based on a combination of next-token and attribute likelihoods. Our approach achieves state of the art performance on sparse reward tasks, improves next-token prediction at sufficient model sizes, estimates attribute probabilities orders of magnitude faster than sampling, and can guide decoding of autoregressive sequence models on a range of language tasks.

</details>


### [167] [Sheaf-Theoretic Transport and Obstruction for Detecting Scientific Theory Shift in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14033)
*David N. Olivieri,Roque J. Hernández*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于有限层论（finite sheaf-theoretic）的框架，用于检测AI智能体中的科学理论转移候选。通过组织源、重叠、目标、验证图表并测试它们的粘合性，框架能够识别表征框架何时因局部-全局阻塞而需要扩展，并通过残差拟合、重叠不兼容、约束违反等指标对理论转移候选进行直接障碍排序。实验在受控过渡卡片基准上验证了该方法能有效区分变形与扩展，且目标转移类型的障碍值最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体进行科学理论转移时，仅仅将方程拟合到数据是不够的，需要能够检测现有表征框架能否迁移到新领域，或者是否需要扩展。当前缺乏一个能够形式化描述并检测这种"表征转移失败"的计算框架。

Method: 采用有限层论框架，将上下文组织为源、重叠、目标、验证图表的局部-全局结构；通过拟合、限制和粘合测试来检测表征的传输与阻塞；使用残差拟合、重叠不兼容性、约束违反、极限关系失败和表征成本五个指标来度量阻塞程度。

Result: 在受控过渡卡片基准上，目标变形或扩展通常是障碍值最低的候选；不同转移类型在基准上能够被有效分离；直接障碍排序方法优于仅作为辅助探测手段的星座核方法。

Conclusion: 该研究并非旨在重构历史范式转换或解决开放式的自主理论发明问题，而是为AI智能体隔离出一个有限的诊断子问题：检测表征传输何时失败，以及扩展何时成为连贯的下一步。

Abstract: Scientific theory shift in AI agents requires more than fitting equations to data. An artificial scientific agent must detect whether an existing representational framework remains transportable into a new regime, or whether its language has become locally-to-globally obstructed and must be extended. This paper develops a finite sheaf-theoretic framework for detecting theory-shift candidates through transport and obstruction. Contexts are organized as a local-to-global structure in which source, overlap, target, and validation charts are fitted, restricted, and tested for gluing. Obstruction measures failure of coherence through residual fit, overlap incompatibility, constraint violation, limiting-relation failure, and representational cost. We evaluate the framework on a controlled transition-card benchmark designed to separate deformation within a source language from extension of that language. The main result is direct obstruction ranking: the intended deformation or extension is usually the lowest-obstruction candidate, and transition type is separated in the benchmark. A constellation kernel over the same signatures is included only as a secondary representational-similarity probe. The aim is not to reconstruct historical paradigm shifts or solve open-ended autonomous theory invention, but to isolate a finite diagnostic subproblem for AI agents: detecting when representational transport fails and extension becomes the coherent next move.

</details>


### [168] [Enhanced and Efficient Reasoning in Large Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14036)
*Leslie G. Valiant*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效的、有原则的推理方法，通过将数据重编码为Unary Relational Integracode（一元关系整合码），使大语言模型能够以可计算负担的方式实现可靠的逻辑推理和世界建模，并在多项式时间内可学习核心关系规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型虽然能生成流畅文本，但缺乏有原则的基础来证明其生成内容的可信度。业界普遍认为通过增加更 principled 的推理来解决此问题在计算上不可负担，本文旨在打破这一认知。

Method: 两阶段方法：第一阶段将数据预处理为Unary Relational Integracode，显式编码文本中对象间的关系；第二阶段采用标准的机器学习流程（可精简），学习预测这些关系。该方法可被视为实现了一个世界模型，并推广到视觉、动作等领域。

Result: 该重编码具有关键性质：在保持简洁性的同时，使训练数据中描述世界的关系规则的核心子集能够在多项式时间内可学习（复杂度取决于规则本身），支持单次和多次分类器调用间的可靠推理。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持现有软硬件基础的同时，为LLM提供了有原则且高效的推理能力，弥补了流畅性与可信度之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: In current Large Language Models we can trust the production of smoothly flowing prose on the basis of the principles of machine learning. However, there is no comparably principled basis to justify trust in the content of the text produced. It appears to be conventional wisdom that addressing this issue by adding more principled reasoning is not computationally affordable.
  Here we propose a principled method of reasoning that is efficient enough to be practical for large language models. Further, the method allows the retention of much of the currently used software and hardware base. Our method for improving the functioning of large language models consists of a first stage of preprocessing that recodes the data to a Unary Relational Integracode that is more explicit about the relationships among the objects described in the text, followed as a second stage by a standard but possibly streamlined machine learning process that then also learns to predict these relationships.
  The method may be viewed as realizing a world model and applying beyond natural language, to vision and actions, for example, where the multiple properties of an object referred to in an input are brought together explicitly, rather than remaining distributed in the various references to it in the input. We articulate its advantages in terms of Robust Logic, a system for performing principled chaining on learned, and hence uncertain, information. We show that this recoding has the surprising and fortuitous property that, while succinct, it makes the task of learning a core subset of relational rules that hold in the world described in the training data polynomial time learnable in a defined sense, the polynomial depending on the complexity of the rule. This gives support for sound reasoning within each single call of the learned classifier as well as between multiple calls.

</details>


### [169] [Model-Adaptive Tool Necessity Reveals the Knowing-Doing Gap in LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14038)
*Yize Cheng,Chenrui Fan,Mahdi JafariRaviz,Keivan Rezaei,Soheil Feiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型自适应的工具必要性定义，发现LLM在实际工具调用中存在26.5-54.0%的匹配偏差，并通过内部认知与执行动作的两阶段分解揭示了"知道-行动"差距（knowing-doing gap）是导致工具使用不匹配的主要原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将工具必要性视为与模型无关的属性（由人类或LLM判断标注），忽略了不同模型能力边界差异导致同一问题对强模型无需工具而对弱模型仍需工具的现实情况。

Method: 提出基于每个模型经验表现的模型自适应工具必要性定义；在算术和事实性QA数据集上对比四个模型的必要性标签与实际工具调用行为；将工具使用分解为内部认知阶段（模型是否认为需要工具）和执行阶段（模型是否实际调用工具），通过探针分析隐藏状态并追踪两阶段轨迹。

Result: 发现必要性标签与实际工具调用存在26.5-54.0%（算术）和30.8-41.8%（事实QA）的偏差；内部认知和执行信号均可线性解码，但在驱动下一步token的末层末位token区域两者探针方向几乎正交；大多数偏差集中在认知到行动的转换阶段，而非认知本身。

Conclusion: LLM工具使用存在"知道-行动"差距：提升工具使用可靠性不仅需要更好的工具必要性识别能力，还需要更有效地将识别结果转化为实际动作。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly act as autonomous agents that must decide when to answer directly vs. when to invoke external tools. Prior work studying adaptive tool use has largely treated tool necessity as a model-agnostic property, annotated by human or LLM judge, and mostly cover cases where the answer is obvious (e.g., fetching the weather vs. paraphrasing text). However, tool necessity in the wild is more nuanced due to the divergence of capability boundaries across models: a problem solvable by a strong model on its own may still require tools for a weaker one. In this work, we introduce a model-adaptive definition of tool-necessity, grounded in each model's empirical performance. Following this definition, we compare the necessity against observed tool-call behavior across four models on arithmetic and factual QA dataset, and find substantial mismatches of 26.5-54.0% and 30.8-41.8%, respectively. To diagnose the failure, we decompose tool use into two stages: an internal cognition stage that reflects whether a model believes a tool is necessary, and an execution stage that determines whether the model actually makes a tool-call action. By probing the LLM hidden states, we find that both signals are often linearly decodable, yet their probe directions become nearly orthogonal in the late-layer, last-token regime that drives the next-token action. By tracing the trajectory of samples in the two-stage process, we further discover that the majority of mismatch is concentrated in the cognition-to-action transition, not in cognition itself. These results reveal a knowing-doing gap in LLM tool-use: improving tool-use reliability requires not only better recognition of when tools are needed, but also better translation of that recognition into action.

</details>


### [170] [Bridging Legal Interpretation and Formal Logic: Faithfulness, Assumption, and the Future of AI Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14049)
*Olivia Peiyu Wang,Leilani H. Gilpin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种神经符号方法，将大语言模型的表达能力与形式化验证的严谨性相结合，以解决法律AI中LLM系统性地进行超越源文本支持的推断问题，旨在实现既强大又可信的AI辅助法律推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在法律实践中存在系统性过度推断问题——不仅捏造事实和引用，更严重的是源文本之外的假设性结论被呈现为逻辑推理结果，而法律领域的高风险特性要求严格的验证标准，当前AI系统无法满足这一需求。

Method: 神经符号方法（Neuro-symbolic approach）：结合大语言模型的表达能力和形式化验证的严谨性，在保持LLM灵活性的同时引入可验证的推理约束。

Result: 未在摘要中给出具体实验结果，但目标是减少人工验证负担，同时保持法律实践所需的问责性。

Conclusion: 该方案有望使AI辅助法律推理既具备能力又值得信赖，在不牺牲问责性的前提下降低手动验证负担。

Abstract: The growing adoption of large language models in legal practice brings both significant promise and serious risk. Legal professionals stand to benefit from AI that can reason over contracts, draft documents, and analyze sources at scale, yet the high-stakes nature of legal work demands a level of rigor that current AI systems do not provide. The central problem is not simply that LLMs hallucinate facts and references; it is that they systematically draw inferences that go beyond what the source text actually supports, presenting assumption-laden conclusions as if they were logically grounded. This proposal presents a neuro-symbolic approach to legal AI that combines the expressive power of large language models with the rigor of formal verification, aiming to make AI-assisted legal reasoning both capable and trustworthy, thus reducing the burden of manual verification without sacrificing the accountability that legal practice demands.

</details>


### [171] [SPIN: Structural LLM Planning via Iterative Navigation for Industrial Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14051)
*Yusuke Ozaki,Dhaval Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPIN是一个结合了有向无环图（DAG）规划验证与基于前缀的执行控制的规划包装器，能减少无效任务执行并提升LLM agent系统的规划效率与正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM规划器常产生结构无效或不必要过长的流程，导致脆性失败和API成本浪费，工业agent系统亟需一种可强制执行结构有效性并提前终止冗余执行的轻量级解决方案。

Method: SPIN包含两个核心机制：(1) 通过_validate_plan_text和修复性提示强制严格的DAG契约，确保计划在执行前结构有效；(2) 增量评估DAG前缀，在当前前缀足以回答查询时提前停止执行。

Result: 在AssetOpsBench的261个场景中，SPIN将执行任务从1061降至623，Accomplished从0.638提升至0.706，每次运行的API调用从11.81降至6.82。在MCP Bench上，SPIN同时提升了GPT OSS1和Llama 4 Maverick在规划、接地和依赖相关的评分。

Conclusion: SPIN通过结构验证与智能提前终止，在不改变底层LLM的情况下显著提升规划质量和执行效率，是一种通用且轻量的规划包装方案。

Abstract: Industrial LLM agent systems often separate planning from execution, yet LLM planners frequently produce structurally invalid or unnecessarily long workflows, leading to brittle failures and avoidable tool and API cost. We propose \texttt{SPIN}, a planning wrapper that combines validated Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) planning with prefix based execution control. \texttt{SPIN} enforces a strict DAG contract through \texttt{\_validate\_plan\_text} and repair prompting, producing executable plans before downstream execution, and then evaluates DAG prefixes incrementally to stop when the current prefix is sufficient to answer the query. On AssetOpsBench, across 261 scenarios, \texttt{SPIN} reduces executed tasks from 1061 to 623 and improves \emph{Accomplished} from 0.638 to 0.706, while reducing tool calls from 11.81 to 6.82 per run. On MCP Bench, the same wrapper improves planning, grounding, and dependency related scores for both GPT OSS1 and Llama 4 Maverick.

</details>


### [172] [MathAtlas: A Benchmark for Autoformalization in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14061)
*Nilay Patel,Noah Arias,Davit Babayan,Victoria Cochran,Timothy Libman,Hafsah Mahmood,Liam McCarty,Soli Munoz,Laurel Willey,Jeffrey Flanigan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MathAtlas是首个大规模研究生级数学自动形式化基准，包含约5.2万个来自103本研究生数学教材的定理、定义等内容，配有约17.8万条数学依赖关系图。实验表明该基准质量高但极具挑战性，最强基线在定理陈述上仅达9.8%正确率，在深度依赖子集上最佳模型仅2.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动形式化基准主要集中在奥林匹克或本科数学层面，研究生级和研究级数学尚未被充分探索，亟需一个覆盖更高层次数学的基准来推动该领域发展。

Method: 从103本研究生数学教材中提取约5.2万个定理、定义、习题、示例和证明，构建了包含约17.8万条关系的数学依赖图，并基于依赖深度划分出MA-Hard子集用于评估。

Result: 强基线模型在定理陈述上正确率不超过9.8%，定义上不超过16.7%；在依赖深度最大的MA-Hard子集上，最佳模型仅达2.6%正确率；模型性能随依赖深度增加而显著下降。

Conclusion: MathAtlas作为首个包含依赖关系的大规模研究生级数学自动形式化基准，揭示了当前模型在深层次数学依赖场景下的严重不足，为未来开发依赖感知的自动形式化系统提供了重要评估平台。

Abstract: Current autoformalization benchmarks are largely focused on olympiad or undergraduate mathematics, while graduate and research-level mathematics remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce MathAtlas, the first large-scale autoformalization benchmark of in the wild graduate-level mathematics, containing ~52k theorems, definitions, exercises, examples, and proofs extracted from 103 graduate mathematics textbooks. MathAtlas is enriched with a mathematical dependency graph containing ~178k relations, and is the first autoformalization benchmark to include such relations, facilitating evaluation and development of dependency-aware autoformalization systems. Our extensive experiments show that MathAtlas is high quality but extremely challenging: strong baselines achieve at most 9.8% correctness on theorem statements and 16.7% on definitions. Furthermore, we find performance of state-of-the-art models degrades substantially with dependency depth: on MA-Hard, a subset of 700 entities with the deepest dependency trees, the best model achieves only 2.6% correctness for autoformalization on this challenging dataset. We release MathAtlas to the community as a benchmark set for large-scale autoformalization of graduate-level mathematics in the wild.

</details>


### [173] [Know When To Fold 'Em: Token-Efficient LLM Synthetic Data Generation via Multi-Stage In-Flight Rejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14062)
*Anjir Ahmed Chowdhury,Syed Zawad,Feng Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多阶段飞行拒绝（MSIFR）框架，通过中间检查点提前终止低质量生成轨迹，在不牺牲质量的前提下将token消耗降低11%-77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据生成流程通常先生成完整输出再过滤，导致大量token浪费在被丢弃的低质量样本上。

Method: 将生成过程分解为多个阶段，在每个阶段使用基于规则的快速验证器检测算术不一致、幻觉模式和格式违规，早期拒绝有缺陷样本。形式上建模为序贯决策过程，并证明任何非平凡丢弃策略都能降低期望token消耗，且条件效用估计构成鞅，确保飞行拒绝不偏置保留样本的期望效用。

Result: 在5个指令微调模型和7个推理基准上，MSIFR单独使用降低token消耗11%-77%，结合早期退出方法可降低78.2%，同时保持或提升评估准确率。

Conclusion: MSIFR提供了一种无需额外训练或架构修改的轻量级、即插即用框架，有效提升LLM合成数据生成的效率。

Abstract: While synthetic data generation with large language models (LLMs) is widely used in post-training pipelines, existing approaches typically generate full outputs before applying quality filters, leading to substantial token waste on samples that are ultimately discarded. To address this, we propose Multi-Stage In-Flight Rejection (MSIFR), a lightweight, training-free framework that detects and terminates low-quality generation trajectories at intermediate checkpoints before they reach full completion. MSIFR decomposes the generation process into sequential stages and applies fast rule-based validators to identify arithmetic inconsistencies, hallucination patterns, and formatting violations, enabling early rejection of faulty samples. We formalize in-flight rejection as a sequential decision process and show that any non-trivial discard policy reduces expected token consumption, with stage-wise savings increasing when rejection occurs earlier in the generation pipeline. We further demonstrate that conditional utility estimates form a martingale, ensuring that early, in-flight rejection does not bias the expected utility of retained samples. Across five instruction-tuned models and seven reasoning benchmarks, MSIFR reduces token consumption by 11%-77% as a standalone method, and up to 78.2% when combined with early-exit methods, while preserving or improving evaluation accuracy. These results confirm that MSIFR provides a practical mechanism for improving the efficiency of LLM-based synthetic data generation without additional training or architectural changes.

</details>


### [174] [SkillFlow: Flow-Driven Recursive Skill Evolution for Agentic Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14089)
*Mingda Zhang,Tiesunlong Shen,Haoran Luo,Wenjin Liu,Zikai Xiao,Erik Cambria,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillFlow是一个基于流的框架，采用可训练的Supervisor作为智能体，通过Tempered Trajectory Balance（TTB）回归式流匹配损失、透明信用分配和递归技能进化机制，在14个数据集上显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的编排方法面临三大挑战：策略在奖励最大化下崩溃、梯度方差高且信用分配不透明、技能进化缺乏原则性训练信号指导。

Method: 提出SkillFlow框架，包含：(1) Tempered Trajectory Balance (TTB)回归式流匹配损失，按奖励比例采样轨迹以保持策略多样性；(2) 联合学习的后向策略实现零额外推理成本的逐步骤透明信用分配；(3) 递归技能进化机制，基于流诊断信号自主决定何时进化、创建或剪枝技能。

Result: 在14个数据集上，SkillFlow在问答、数学推理、代码生成和真实世界交互式决策任务中显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: SkillFlow通过基于流的训练信号闭环，实现了从训练信号到自主能力增长的自动化任务编排，有效解决了现有方法的策略崩溃、信用分配不透明和技能进化无指导等关键问题。

Abstract: In recent years, a variety of powerful LLM-based agentic systems have been applied to automate complex tasks through task orchestration. However, existing orchestration methods still face key challenges, including strategy collapse under reward maximization, high gradient variance with opaque credit assignment, and unguided skill evolution whose decisions are typically made by directly prompting an LLM to judge rather than derived from principled training signals. To address these challenges, we propose SkillFlow, a flow-based framework that takes a trainable Supervisor as the agent and a structured environment with dynamic skill library and frozen executor, automating task orchestration through multi-turn interaction. SkillFlow employs Tempered Trajectory Balance (TTB), a regression-based flow-matching loss that samples trajectories proportional to reward, preserving diverse orchestration strategies rather than collapsing to a single mode. The same flow objective yields a jointly learned backward policy that provides transparent per-step credit assignment at zero additional inference cost. Building on these flow diagnostics, a recursive skill evolution mechanism determines when to evolve, what skills to create or prune, and where decision gaps lie -- closing the loop from training signal to autonomous capability growth. Experimental results on 14 datasets show that SkillFlow significantly outperforms baselines across question answering, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and real-world interactive decision making tasks. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SkillFlow-E850.

</details>


### [175] [ClawForge: Generating Executable Interactive Benchmarks for Command-Line Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14133)
*Yuxiang Lai,Peng Xia,Haonian Ji,Kaiwen Xiong,Kaide Zeng,Jiaqi Liu,Fang Wu,Jike Zhong,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ClawForge是一个基于生成器的基准框架，用于评估智能体在存在状态冲突的命令行工作流中的执行能力。该框架通过情景模板、初始化状态、参考轨迹和验证器生成可复现的任务规范，并基于最终状态和副作用而非精确轨迹匹配进行评估。在17个场景、6个能力类别上的评估表明，最佳模型严格准确率仅45.3%，错误状态替换率低于17%，模型间最大差异（17%-90%）取决于智能体是否在执行前检查现有状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式基准测试面临可扩展构建与真实工作流评估之间的张力：手工构建任务扩展和修改成本高，静态提示评估无法捕捉持久状态下的失败模式，且大多数基准从干净状态初始化任务，未系统测试智能体处理预存在的部分、过期或冲突工件的能力。

Method: 提出ClawForge框架，基于生成器将情景模板、填充槽位、初始化状态、参考轨迹和验证器编译为可复现的任务规范，采用归一化最终状态和可观察副作用而非精确轨迹匹配进行逐步评估。实例化为ClawForge-Bench（17个场景，6个能力类别）。

Result: 七种前沿模型测试结果显示：最佳模型严格准确率仅45.3%，错误状态替换率低于17%，模型间最大差异（17%-90%）由是否在执行前检查现有状态决定。部分积分和步骤效率分析表明多数失败是接近关闭而非早期崩溃，且模型在状态冲突下表现出不同失败风格。

Conclusion: ClawForge有效揭示了现有模型在持久状态工作流中的能力局限，特别是状态检查行为对成败的关键影响，以及不同模型在状态冲突下的差异化失败模式，为交互式智能体评估提供了更真实的测试范式。

Abstract: Interactive agent benchmarks face a tension between scalable construction and realistic workflow evaluation. Hand-authored tasks are expensive to extend and revise, while static prompt evaluation misses failures that only appear when agents operate over persistent state. Existing interactive benchmarks have advanced agent evaluation significantly, but most initialize tasks from clean state and do not systematically test how agents handle pre-existing partial, stale, or conflicting artifacts. We present \textbf{ClawForge}, a generator-backed benchmark framework for executable command-line workflows under state conflict. The framework compiles scenario templates, grounded slots, initialized state, reference trajectories, and validators into reproducible task specifications, and evaluates agents step by step over persistent workflow surfaces using normalized end state and observable side effects rather than exact trajectory matching. We instantiate this framework as the ClawForge-Bench (17 scenarios, 6 ability categories). Results across seven frontier models show that the best model reaches only 45.3% strict accuracy, wrong-state replacement remains below 17\% for all models, and the widest model separation (17% to 90%) is driven by whether agents inspect existing state before acting. Partial-credit and step-efficiency analyses further reveal that many failures are near-miss closures rather than early breakdowns, and that models exhibit qualitatively different failure styles under state conflict.

</details>


### [176] [Distribution-Aware Algorithm Design with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14141)
*Saharsh Koganti,Priyadarsi Mishra,Pierfrancesco Beneventano,Tomer Galanti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究从任务分布中学习可执行求解器代码（而非预测器），提出"求解器提示"抽象——从样本中提取可复用结构并编译为特化求解器代码。理论上证明了固定库中经验最快样本一致求解器的正确性与运行时间泛化性，以及统计可识别提示的可恢复性。实验上，用LLM代码智能体在7类问题的21个组合优化分布上合成求解器，达到0.971平均归一化质量，相比最优启发式提升0.098，相比质量最优启发式、Gurobi和限时精确求解器分别快336.9倍、342.8倍和16.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习关注预测器的准确率，但在求解器代码场景中，正确性不足以保证实用性——不同求解器可能都返回有效解，但运行时间差异巨大。现有方法未能系统地从样本中学习可复用的结构信息以加速求解。

Method: 提出"求解器提示"（solver hint）抽象，从样本中提取可重用结构并编译为特化代码；理论分析固定库中经验最快样本一致求解器的泛化界；利用LLM代码智能体实例化该框架，从目标分布样本中合成求解器代码。

Result: 在7类问题的21个组合优化分布上，合成求解器平均归一化质量0.971，比平均启发式池高0.224，比最优启发式高0.098；运行时间比质量最优启发式快336.9倍，比Gurobi快342.8倍，比限时精确求解器快16.1倍。在PACE 2025 Dominating Set私有实例上，对所有100个图有效，速度比顶级竞赛求解器快约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 本文证明学习可执行求解器代码是可行的，且能同时保证正确性和运行时间效率。性能提升的关键在于将通用指数搜索或通用优化替换为基于分布特化的计算，从算法层面改变计算规模。

Abstract: We study learning when the learned object is executable solver code rather than a predictor. In this setting, correctness is not enough: two solvers may both return valid solutions on the deployment distribution while differing substantially in runtime. Given samples from an unknown task distribution, the learner returns code evaluated on fresh instances by both solution quality and execution time. Our central abstraction is a \emph{solver hint}: reusable structure inferred from samples and compiled into specialized solver code. We prove that the empirically fastest sample-consistent solver from a fixed library generalizes in both correctness and runtime, and that statistically identifiable hints can be recovered and compiled from polynomially many samples.
  Empirically, we instantiate the framework with LLM code agents on \(21\) structured combinatorial-optimization target distributions across seven problem classes. The synthesized solvers reach mean normalized quality \(0.971\), improve by \(+0.224\) over the average heuristic pool and by \(+0.098\) over the highest-quality heuristic, and are \(336.9\times\), \(342.8\times\), and \(16.1\times\) faster than the quality-best heuristic, Gurobi, and the selected time-limited exact backend, respectively. On released PACE 2025 Dominating Set private instances, the synthesized solver is valid on all \(100\) graphs and runs about two orders of magnitude faster than top competition solvers, with a moderate quality gap. Inspection shows that many gains come from changing the computational scale: replacing ambient exponential search or general-purpose optimization with compiled distribution-specific computation.

</details>


### [177] [Agentic Systems as Boosting Weak Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14163)
*Varun Sunkaraneni,Pierfrancesco Beneventano,Riccardo Neumarker,Tomaso Poggio,Tomer Galanti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于验证器支持的委员会搜索（verifier-backed committee search）作为推理语言模型的推理时增强方法。通过使用弱推理模型（GPT-5.4 nano）的多次采样和批评-比较器编排，在SWE-bench Verified上达到了76.4%的正确率，与强模型（Gemini 3 Pro和Claude Opus 4.5 Thinking）性能相当，接近79.0%的oracle best-of-8上限。理论分析表明，覆盖度可通过重复采样放大，但可靠的放大需要局部可验证信号（如执行、证明检查等）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究是否可以用多个弱推理模型的委员会达到强模型的表现，并理解其背后的机制与局限性。

Method: 提出verifier-backed committee search框架，将过程分解为proposal覆盖度、局部可识别性、进展和多样性四个维度。理论分析了覆盖度放大与局部可验证信号的必要性，并给出了基于排序的误差传播界。实验采用GPT-5.4 nano作为proposer，通过critic-comparator编排从k=8个候选方案中选择。

Result: 单个GPT-5.4 nano解决67.0%的任务；通过critic-comparator编排在k=8时达到76.4%，匹配Gemini 3 Pro和Claude Opus 4.5 Thinking的独立性能，接近79.0%的oracle best-of-8上界。剩余失败主要是proposal覆盖度失败。

Conclusion: 弱模型生成的候选池中已包含大量正确方案，主要挑战在于选择而非生成。当前方法的性能上限受限于proposal覆盖度（即模型共享的盲区），更强的选择机制无法解决覆盖度问题。

Abstract: Can a committee of weak reasoning-model calls reach the performance of much stronger models? We study verifier-backed committee search as inference-time boosting for reasoning language models. The mechanism is not simply that ``more agents help'': samples expose latent correct solutions, while critics and comparators must recover them without access to the hidden verifier. We formalize this view by separating proposal coverage, local identifiability, progress, and diversity. We prove that coverage can be amplified by repeated sampling, but cannot by itself create useful critics or comparators; reliable amplification requires an additional local soundness signal, such as execution, proof checking, type checking, tests, or constraint solving. We give rank-based bounds showing when local selection errors compose into reliable trajectories, and characterize the proposer-side ceiling: oracle best-of-\(k\) converges only to the mass of task slices on which the proposal system assigns nonzero useful probability. Empirically, on SWE-bench Verified, a single \texttt{GPT-5.4 nano} proposal solves \(67.0\%\) of tasks. Using the same nano model, our critic--comparator orchestration reaches \(76.4\%\) with \(k=8\) proposals, matching the standalone performance of \texttt{Gemini 3 Pro} and \texttt{Claude Opus 4.5} Thinking and approaching the \(79.0\%\) oracle best-of-\(8\) upper bound. Thus, many correct patches are already present in weak-model proposal pools; the main challenge is selecting them. The remaining failures are mostly proposal-coverage failures, indicating shared blind spots that stronger selection alone cannot close.

</details>


### [178] [Unsteady Metrics and Benchmarking Cultures of AI Model Builders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14164)
*Stefan Baack,Christo Buschek,Maty Bohacek*

Main category: cs.AI
